Thanks to the help of our scorekeeper who crunched the numbers for us, we ended up with a complete list of all 3-0 drafters from the first draft of the day. Don't worry if you find it a little hard to interpret all of this data because we'll do that for you in another article when we break down all of the popular cards from these decklists!
